- [F179](#f179) (2026-11-20): #1876's W6 round trip is fixed in the same PR: .dtrunc (and .dround if cheap) map the interval monotonically in the transfer; .decimal's transfer stays id; no D-number.
- [F178](#f178) (2026-11-20): #1873 (exact limb state) is signed with condition R1 (attach checks zero-count and group-membership invariants) and changes R2-R4 (drop dead b=10 split step, keep rebuild figure, fix wording).
- [F176](#f176) (2026-11-20): A1 for Arrow-fed sources takes (b): one store-copy path for every source, Source docstring corrected, measurement with a minimal table-free source; io.Source's held table counts as held by sverify. (a) recorded for later. Clamp case closed. Amended by [U-2026-11-20-io-source-gate](#u-2026-11-20-io-source-gate).
- [F173](#f173) (2026-11-20): The pruned copy of an Arrow-fed input is a State of the engine's Store (the maintained view project S t), fixed at a seal after the view is made; refuse at registration where a snapshot is missing, never keep-all. StaticSource keeps its API. No D-number. Amended by [F176](#f176).
- [F169](#f169) (2026-11-19): Withdraws K5's side lemma; Rel.floatOpenSafeP_of_noDouble takes the decidable hypothesis r.closedB = true, the gate checks it, and integer BIT_* and QUANTILE_DISC plans are refused; widening closedB to .bit is a later PR.
- [F166](#f166) (2026-11-19): Bind moves to the frontend as Sverify.Frontend.Bind; Outcome.bind_error, Outcome.ok_bind and Expr.evalP_subst move unchanged to core Profile.lean; Expr.readsFieldFrom/readsField stay in Parse.lean. No D-number.
- [F159](#f159) (2026-11-19): #900's Python step is approved in direction: refinement and plan bound move into Realize.lean, the checker emits the final class with coded problems, Python keeps wording; acceptance is a site count of zero, one PR in two commits, C1 to C6.
- [F154](#f154) (2026-11-19): Track B's F3 is approved: plans with no double transfer to the reference via Rel.transfer_of_noDouble under transferB on t.refAt floatOnlyOptions; one mayDouble/hasDouble family in core; no extras hypothesis; raisesOn in #1834 accepted. Amended by [F169](#f169), [F171](#f171).
- [F153](#f153) (2026-11-19): Accepts P1b's class-level predicate Relation.DoublesKinded and the bridge lemma; core has the typing fact (Rel.kinds_sound), so the discharge is follow-up P1c; ObsCtx.ofRelation is dropped in favour of an example. Amended by [F154](#f154).
- [F152](#f152) (2026-11-19): Rewrites apply wherever their verification holds: under semantics='duckdb' no rule theorem changes and the gate opens in RunPlan.lean (R1); pending profiles use the reference's gate; engine profiles need a refinement under one table after #271. Amended by [F217](#f217).
- [F151](#f151) (2026-11-19): Approves #1838's Lean move once M1 to M5 are met (repair docs/proofs.md, two test assertions, IssuesAll in the no-engine test, Refresh to analysis, two links); import-free modules are placed by their subject.
- [F149](#f149) (2026-11-19): #1828 keeps its scope; P1b follows as its own PR, strengthening check_deterministic_sound and check_class_determined_sound from OrderAllowed to Allowed with DeterminedUpTo corollaries (Obs.members, Obs.classes); no FloatOpenSafe side condition.
- [F148](#f148) (2026-11-19): Approves the #1444 root-move plan: Completeness goes to core, ProvedOn/Statements/IssuesAll stay in the root, OpenSums is split later (O2), and the acceptance becomes the ROOT_CHECKER/ROOT_THEOREMS/ROOT_WAITING sets in test_lean_layers.py. Amended by [F151](#f151).
- [F146](#f146) (2026-11-19): AVG leaves #1805, which states the corollary for a double SUM only; SumNear for AVG stays a named hypothesis; an unconditional AVG lemma (B1, B2) goes with track A's ruling 135 (b) follow-up.
- [F143](#f143) (2026-11-19): Approves the #1443 registry plan with five changes: keep the name EngineProfile for now (rename to ProfileRows later), side is a field recorded as sideName and printed, EngineEntry.rows, out-of-tree engines state an entry at their next pin bump.
- [F136](#f136) (2026-11-19): The optimizer's near gate may narrow to plans where a double can occur, if the check covers every expression, runs on s and r, names the open `FloatOpenSafeP` obligation and per-engine theorems; else it stays as in 129. Amended by [F150](#f150), [F154](#f154).
- [F134](#f134) (2026-11-19): #1795 unifies `Rel.keyedJoin` for Keys, Rows and AnalysisCongr only; `joinElim` and `eagerAgg` stay key-only, range joins there go to the #1787 backlog after step 2.
- [F130](#f130) (2026-11-19): Track B #1484: F1 builds issues on every allowed order with `DataAssumed` taking `SmallAll`; F2 keeps the black-box clamp (no axiom, no D56), the checker reports law breaks and laws; F3 builds root-aggregate `FloatOpenSafeP`. Amended by [F135](#f135), [F140](#f140).
- [F129](#f129) (2026-11-19): #1787 step 2 built with eight conditions: `Rule` enum with per-rule default, exact ORDER BY shape for sequence, issue gate with per-rule `keepsIssues`, reads/functions subsets, reference profile only, foreign keys in the statement, `Small` per rule, one plan accessor. Amended by [F136](#f136).
- [F127](#f127) (2026-11-19): #1787 step 1 accepted: congruence lemmas for refinement in `Refines.lean`, `Engine.Sound.of_refines` and `contract_sound_rewrite`, `Engine.runPlan`; conditions: move `Measure.Allowed.perm` to core, document per-constructor lemma forms, add `contract_sound_runs_rewrite`, weaken `check` hypothesis. Lean only.
- [F120](#f120) (2026-11-19): Staging `Rel.evalChoices_allowed` behind a decidable `Rel.ChoicesSupported` is accepted with four conditions: PR 1 carries ruling 113 part 0, the predicate lives only in the theorem, each later PR removes one case, PR 2 handles aggregates with open cells.
- [F117](#f117) (2026-11-19): Measure.openB is obsolete after D25 and its open-sum machinery is unreachable; delete it (openB, aggCtx, near_eval, floatOpenSafe_aggregate and the postAllowed branch) in a small Lean PR separate from #1749.
- [F116](#f116) (2026-11-19): #1329's join line measures the join sides kept to match, not the materialized output; amendments: Arrow-fed inputs leave the driver, R2/R3 are one projection lemma over Z-sets, lake sources need a pinned-snapshot precondition. Amended by [F173](#f173).
- [F113](#f113) (2026-11-19): Rel.evalChoices: first fix exact-equality grouping/dedup to distinctRows; prove acceptance implies allowed (Rel.evalChoices_allowed, val matches by =, multiset); the proposed allowed-implies-accepted direction is false and recorded as the oracle's known gap. Amended by [F120](#f120).
- [F108](#f108) (2026-11-19): 12d-4: keys reading tainted columns are a `source_threshold` site (rule C-key); the S2(c) control is restated and `ratioSharpen` for marked quotients is a follow-up with a decidable side condition. D52 gains 'and keys'.
- [F102](#f102) (2026-11-19): D50-d design second part: one running three-valued column per guarded chain, levels pruned via emit, binder as a pure Lean frontend function with theorem T1; join/semi conditions and lambda bodies keep d1's chain and cap at 1.0; engines re-probed (R1-R4). Amended by [U-2026-11-19-d50-cap](#u-2026-11-19-d50-cap).
- [F95](#f95) (2026-11-19): D50-d3: the linear shape is the guarded column, each chain operand bound once in a projection with a reach column; no core form; d1's encoding stands as ruled in 94; design filed as issue #1719. Amended by [F102](#f102).
- [F94](#f94) (2026-11-19): D50-d inputs: step d1 rewrites the guard chain as CASE WHEN NOT p THEN FALSE ELSE p AND (...) END (two copies only where a raising operand follows); no checker sharing for 1.0; linear size undecided pending d1's table and a profile (d2). Amended by [F102](#f102), [F95](#f95).
- [U-2026-11-19-rewrites-every-profile](#u-2026-11-19-rewrites-every-profile) (2026-11-19): Proved rewrites apply under every profile whose analysis proves their preconditions, not only under the reference.
- [U-2026-11-19-exact-limb-state](#u-2026-11-19-exact-limb-state) (2026-11-19): Maintained double SUM, AVG, VAR, STDDEV, COVAR and CORR stay exact (D25, D30); the state moves into the engines as integer limbs; no compensated and no quantised state.
- [U-2026-11-19-auditability-standing](#u-2026-11-19-auditability-standing) (2026-11-19): Auditability is a standing concern: a bridge lemma, one read-only verdict function in Python, and a trusted-base page with a test.
- [F81](#f81) (2026-11-18): Step 12e confirmed: COUNT's abstract upper bound comes from the input's row cap (`Measure.abs` count case), `abs_sound` keeps its statement; the cap reaches a subquery's COUNT except through relations without `maxRows`.
- [F80](#f80) (2026-11-18): D51 Lean/Python pre-build statements accepted: Text.upper/lower as definitions over the generated table; issue caseDiffers and option case_mapping; facts renamed upperAgreed/lowerAgreed with one per-column kinds list; ILIKE stays under D43's ASCII raise until fold facts exist. Amended by [F83](#f83).
- [F69](#f69) (2026-11-18): A refusal's exit must be a query the user can get proved; step 12e gives COUNT an upper bound from the row cap in Measure.abs. No D-number. Amended by [F70](#f70).
- [F60](#f60) (2026-11-18): #173: N6 (stores) comes before N7 (sinks), and N7 builds on the engine's real handles; Arrow-backed writers (A) and a DuckDB-only sink protocol (C) declined. Parquet-writer facts may start now.
- [F55](#f55) (2026-11-18): Blocker for #1631: regexp_match's row gets its constructor and the #guard checks the transfer's actual read of non_ascii_text instead of naming functions by hand; test a bare regexp_match projection is unproved under semantics='substrait'.
- [F51](#f51) (2026-11-18): A7 Statements.lean: closed moves into engine conditions, a Checked layer under Verified, postcondition as a predicate on results; worked examples are #guards plus pointers to kernel examples; no check_optimized_sound corollary.
- [F50](#f50) (2026-11-18): A6 (PR #1623): check_proved_on and its three engine corollaries accepted; closed stays the float-safety field and underReference stays on the DuckDB corollary; five text conditions. Amended by [F51](#f51).
- [F47](#f47) (2026-11-18): D50: entry 46's chain is replaced by a nested THEN shape (shape A); an analysis change (B) passes condition facts to the THEN branch; a TRUE-only guard is refused.
- [F46](#f46) (2026-11-18): D50: entry 45's shape is withdrawn for a linear CASE chain (sverify_and/sverify_or); plain conjunctions stay byte-identical and each tier-1 engine needs a laziness probe, else the lifted plan is refused. Amended by [F47](#f47).
- [F43](#f43) (2026-11-18): A5 (#1484 finding 4): Rel.FloatOpenSafeP is the open-float hypothesis (not Engine.RefWithin); contract_sound_near is the core step; Engine.Runs loses tested; docs must say nothing discharges the hypothesis yet.
- [F41](#f41) (2026-11-17): looseSub stays on Rel.hasIssue_of_raises, in its own PR before A6; before 1.0 HasIssue reads conditions over loose or padded rows so the theorem covers left joins over filtered subqueries.
- [F40](#f40) (2026-11-17): Entry 33 holds with the corrected count; the seven black-box aggregates (ENTROPY, SEM, PRODUCT, KURTOSIS, KAHAN_SUM, MAD, SKEWNESS) get no order-free follow-up before 1.0, only a report naming them.
- [F39](#f39) (2026-11-17): core/ comments name an engine only at an option's declaration or inside a fact id, decision or issue number; a reference choice cites its decision; core/ describes no engine shim.
- [F35](#f35) (2026-11-17): #1575: Rel.orderAllowed_window's restated form is accepted with no corollary, and winFact_sound and winFacts_sound are generalised; Seam.lean's header names the new form.
- [F34](#f34) (2026-11-17): D45 window statements approved for the build: Rel.OrderAllowed's window case opens as Rel.Allowed's, and the PR builds on #1544's head.
- [F33](#f33) (2026-11-17): Integer AVG (.iavg) enters AggFn.orderFree in a follow-up PR of track A's after #1544 merges, before the next release branch; #1544 ships naming its flips as known.
- [F32](#f32) (2026-11-17): The no-raise premise leaves Engine.Sound and Engine.contract_sound; DuckDB carries it as the named side condition Engine.NoRaiseSide; D16 gets a dated amendment.
- [F27](#f27) (2026-11-17): Native lake scans (#173): one metadata-only scan descriptor; route A (engine Parquet reader) is the 1.0 route; credentials never travel; engines write sink files and sverify commits; state is Parquet; faithfulness is one differential property per engine. Amended by [F53](#f53).
- [F15](#f15) (2026-11-17): #1525: runOn refuses every request whose evaluation panicked (R6 closes with the wrapper, no LEAN_ABORT_ON_PANIC); total black-box evaluators are not part of R6.
- [F10](#f10) (2026-11-17): A2: the reference fixes the comparison for every kind on main, so neither option is taken; the six failing tests keep their expectations and fail for missing conditions 1 and 2 of entry 9.
- [F9](#f9) (2026-11-17): A2: the worker's FETCH counterexample is false, so Rel.Allowed, SortAllowed and D10 stay; rep.deterministic := detPlan is accepted over Rel.Allowed with seven conditions in the same PR. Amended by [F10](#f10).
- [F3](#f3) (2026-11-17): Rel.HasIssue is strengthened so every eager subexpression of a filter or join condition may run on any combination of loose join-input rows (left joins count as cross joins); verdicts must not change. Amended by [F41](#f41).
- [U-2026-11-09-engine-native-state-stores](#u-2026-11-09-engine-native-state-stores) (2026-11-09): The engine a pipeline runs on holds its state natively through its own store, ArrowStore is removed, and the state store is a required plugin part for incremental engines.
- [U-2026-11-09-engine-free-verify-only-base](#u-2026-11-09-engine-free-verify-only-base) (2026-11-09): Every engine, DuckDB included, is an optional extra; base sverify is verify-only, with store, scans, pipeline state and IO behind the engine extras. Amended by [U-2026-11-15-engine-free-base-1-0-gate](#u-2026-11-15-engine-free-base-1-0-gate).
- [U-2026-11-08-semantics-x-is-profile-choice](#u-2026-11-08-semantics-x-is-profile-choice) (2026-11-08): semantics="X" is a choice of profile to verify under, not a frontend-only mode; the note claiming the reference is the only verification semantics is withdrawn.
- [U-2026-11-08-lowerings-lifts-symmetric](#u-2026-11-08-lowerings-lifts-symmetric) (2026-11-08): Lowerings and lifts stay separate machineries, each with one shape across every target engine or source frontend.
- [U-2026-11-08-engine-package-structure-first](#u-2026-11-08-engine-package-structure-first) (2026-11-08): Each engine is one directory with its Lean package, Python distribution, facts and tests, entering through the plugin interface; feature work pauses until the structure exists, wrong-answer bugs excepted. Amended by [U-2026-11-15-structure-freeze-ends](#u-2026-11-15-structure-freeze-ends).
- [U-2026-11-08-engine-behaviour-in-profiles](#u-2026-11-08-engine-behaviour-in-profiles) (2026-11-08): Engine-specific behaviour moves out of the core and the shared path into each engine's profile, dialect, writer or shims.
- [U-2026-11-07-reference-evaluator-conformance-only](#u-2026-11-07-reference-evaluator-conformance-only) (2026-11-07): The Lean reference evaluator serves conformance testing and possibly a Substrait oracle, never as an execution fallback for user plans.
- [U-2026-11-06-core-design-dossier](#u-2026-11-06-core-design-dossier) (2026-11-06): Core design decisions - typed Value as a tagged sum, D25 before 1.0, D26 two NULL equalities, D27 Rel.Small the only size assumption, D28 lexical lambdas, unordered FETCH refused in refresh unless deterministic, every core epic before 1.0.
- [D15](decisions.md#d15-where-the-observations-live) (2026-11-04): One choices table holds each option's class, observation q and shim per profile, so realizability rows come from one table.
- [D7](decisions.md#d7-per-plan-checks) (2026-11-04): Translators are proved once, entry by entry; single plans are checked only for translations sverify does not own.
- [D1](decisions.md#d1-where-the-tables-live) (2026-11-04): Lean holds the option and profile tables; Python mirrors them and a test compares the two.
- [U-2026-11-03-hub-architecture](#u-2026-11-03-hub-architecture) (2026-11-03): The architecture is a hub: a loose core with Substrait, the reference and each engine as refinements.
- [U-2026-11-02-lowering-generated-by-metaprogram](#u-2026-11-02-lowering-generated-by-metaprogram) (2026-11-02): Lowering entries are generated by a Lean metaprogram from the start; hand-written entries only where the search fails.
- [U-2026-11-02-isthmus-dropped](#u-2026-11-02-isthmus-dropped) (2026-11-02): Isthmus and Calcite are dropped as a SQL frontend, leaving SQLGlot and Ibis as the frontends.
- [U-2026-11-02-generated-lift-prioritized](#u-2026-11-02-generated-lift-prioritized) (2026-11-02): Generating the DuckDB SQL lift table from Lean (#268) is prioritized right after the SILENT/SATURATE step of M3; the lift then reads the generated table.
- [U-2026-11-01-frontends-sqlglot-ibis](#u-2026-11-01-frontends-sqlglot-ibis) (2026-11-01): Sverify's frontends are SQLGlot and Ibis, each with sverify's own Substrait producer; Isthmus and DataFusion's SQL planner are removed as frontends. Amended by [U-2026-11-05-datafusion-not-reference](#u-2026-11-05-datafusion-not-reference).

- [F23](#f23) (2026-11-17): S9a list: each of the six guards without a reported kind is classed dead or live in one exact table; a live entry is a wrong verdict fixed in Safety in its own PR.
- [F21](#f21) (2026-11-17): Review: #1525 meets entry 15's three conditions at 6e39605b; no new ruling.
- [F19](#f19) (2026-11-17): #1527 is granted a waiver: the between-members lemma waits for #900, with the lemma listed in the PR body and no doc claiming the bound first.
- [F13](#f13) (2026-11-17): Status report: no ruling needed; #1531 implements entry 3's R3 shape with no verdict change.
- [F11](#f11) (2026-11-17): S9a: no Lean bug and no change to written SQL; a guard on a discharged issue may stay, and the test checks kinds per case and a witness plan per raise_ site, with no exemption table.
- [F2](#f2) (2026-11-16): #1484 round 3 structural findings slotted: S1 (cheap form), S3, S4 (through #1288) and S7 block, S2's label blocks; S2's theorem and raise mode go to wave 3.
- [U-2026-11-16-four-more-reference-pages](#u-2026-11-16-four-more-reference-pages) (2026-11-16): engine-profiles.md, float-bounds.md, optimizations.md and proved-theorems.md are also reference pages.
- [U-2026-11-15-structure-freeze-ends](#u-2026-11-15-structure-freeze-ends) (2026-11-15): The structure-first feature freeze ends with #1189's completion.
- [U-2026-11-15-reference-pages-lean-names](#u-2026-11-15-reference-pages-lean-names) (2026-11-15): Lean names stay only on reference pages (trusted-base.md, reference-semantics.md); other user pages name no Lean lemma, D-number or issue number. Amended by [U-2026-11-16-four-more-reference-pages](#u-2026-11-16-four-more-reference-pages).
- [U-2026-11-08-pr-two-improvements](#u-2026-11-08-pr-two-improvements) (2026-11-08): Every pull request brings two measured improvements beside its main change, one to performance and one to code quality.
- [U-2026-11-04-spec-gaps-on-ledger](#u-2026-11-04-spec-gaps-on-ledger) (2026-11-04): Behaviour the Substrait YAMLs leave unspecified but could express is a spec gap, recorded as an ST-<n> document of kind spec-gap in the PR that meets it.
- [U-2026-11-01-audit-pare-down](#u-2026-11-01-audit-pare-down) (2026-11-01): The user approves the maintainer-tooling audit's recommendations to pare down the package, starting with moving maintainer tooling out of the wheel. Amended by [U-2026-11-01-frontends-sqlglot-ibis](#u-2026-11-01-frontends-sqlglot-ibis).

### Release

- [U-2026-11-22-evidence-gate-bars](#u-2026-11-22-evidence-gate-bars) (2026-11-22): #1487's evidence gate: sverify-dbt's read and proof rate is measured on jaffle_shop and GitLab's public dbt project, and the maintainer sets the bar from the measurement. A person new to sverify, found by the maintainer, gets from `pip install` to a first proved contract from the docs alone in 30 minutes or less. The `sverify_proved` savings are measured on the same two projects and stated in the docs, with no pass mark. Design partners are not a gate.
- [F243](#f243) (2026-11-21): Scope triage of the M2 and M3 milestones: an issue is kept for 1.0 when without it a word the user reads (proved, the version range, the public API, incremental's gate) is false or unbacked; refactors, trust-base reductions and proofs the report does not claim are nice to have or After 1.0. Amended by [U-2026-11-21-complete-lowerings](#u-2026-11-21-complete-lowerings).
- [F237](#f237) (2026-11-21): Scope triage of the 1.0 features milestone under 'comfortably usable over extensive': 13 issues stay 1.0, 13 are nice to have and 12 move After 1.0. Amended by [F239](#f239).
- [F218](#f218) (2026-11-21): Epic-pass rescope: #1484's remaining theorems (epsilon, #271, frontend validation, proof scaling, N10) become stated limits once docs/trusted-base.md names each and the docs' wording matches; #881 S5 and 2.1, #1485 item 9, #891 2.3 and 2.6 stay 1.0; #936 closes as an index. Amended by [F235](#f235).
- [U-2026-11-21-sql-file-dbt-1-0](#u-2026-11-21-sql-file-dbt-1-0) (2026-11-21): Verifying a bare SQL file (#1362) and sverify-dbt (#177) are 1.0 goals.
- [U-2026-11-21-m2-keep](#u-2026-11-21-m2-keep) (2026-11-21): #2008, #886, #1130 and #271 are needed for 1.0, and ruling 243 decides the rest of its confirm calls.
- [U-2026-11-21-land-optimizations](#u-2026-11-21-land-optimizations) (2026-11-21): Optimizations land wherever they can: #1787's item 4 lands before 1.0.
- [U-2026-11-21-composite-keys-1-0](#u-2026-11-21-composite-keys-1-0) (2026-11-21): Composite foreign keys (#2223) are in 1.0.
- [U-2026-11-21-complete-lowerings](#u-2026-11-21-complete-lowerings) (2026-11-21): #271 is complete for 1.0, with no tested-only rows: every `.tracking #271` row of the Polars, Spark and DuckDB tables reaches 0.
- [U-2026-11-21-771-narrowed](#u-2026-11-21-771-narrowed) (2026-11-21): #771 is narrowed for 1.0: the demo flow (steps 1-5) and its acceptance run stay; item 9 narrows to the existing alerts.yml plus a phone-friendly status page per pipeline; item 10 narrows to the HTTP, JSON and CSV sources the Eurostat template uses; general connectors, public-API catalogues, databases and refresh cost and time limits move After 1.0.
- [U-2026-11-21-2245-steps](#u-2026-11-21-2245-steps) (2026-11-21): Steps 0 and 1 of the proposer-API epic #2245 (#2246, #2247) are in 1.0, and the rest of the epic is After 1.0.
- [F215](#f215) (2026-11-20): #1998 merges; incremental maintenance is not reported 1.0-ready until a small change to an Arrow-fed source stops costing a refresh the size of the input (gate items P1-P7).
- [F210](#f210) (2026-11-20): Issue audit: #354's core parameter channel is dropped (After 1.0, with #1098); #2051 closes as covered by D70; `Report.engine_version` is the one version name; #1764 closes for Polars; #1166 closes with a status map.
- [U-2026-11-20-minor-range](#u-2026-11-20-minor-range) (2026-11-20): The user chose the minor range (not exact patch pins) for engine versions; under ruling 199 a probe-run record per engine version, facts keep engine_version.
- [U-2026-11-20-io-source-gate](#u-2026-11-20-io-source-gate) (2026-11-20): On ruling 176's held question, the user chose (b) at 1.0 for the #1329 gate's scope; (a), covering io.Source's held table, is #1945 after 1.0.
- [U-2026-11-20-engine-versions](#u-2026-11-20-engine-versions) (2026-11-20): Every engine is fixed to its one probed minor at 1.0 (DuckDB 2.0.x, Polars 2.1.x, Spark 4.3.x only); unprobed patches in the minor run with a warning and route mark; exact patch pins out. Amended by [U-2026-11-20-minor-range](#u-2026-11-20-minor-range).
- [F141](#f141) (2026-11-19): Audit: CORR's variance-floor bound is after 1.0 (no 1.0 issue); #881 item 2.5 reworded so stability.md puts the plugin interface outside the 1.x promise; drop needs-design from #1807 and #1808, #1810 after rewording (Snowflake details private). Amended by [U-2026-11-19-exact-limb-state](#u-2026-11-19-exact-limb-state).
- [F91](#f91) (2026-11-19): AVG of a decimal of more than 34 digits stays refused for 1.0; #1497 closes on the refusal with conditions W1 to W4; candidates W and S are recorded for the later rule with #271.
- [U-2026-11-19-schedule-1719-323-1285](#u-2026-11-19-schedule-1719-323-1285) (2026-11-19): Guarded AND/OR columns (#1719) and millisecond/nanosecond timestamps (#323) are scheduled for 1.0, and the observables work (#1285) is unblocked.
- [U-2026-11-19-scd2-into-1-0](#u-2026-11-19-scd2-into-1-0) (2026-11-19): SCD2 (#814) moves into 1.0.
- [U-2026-11-19-engine-profiles-pending](#u-2026-11-19-engine-profiles-pending) (2026-11-19): Engine profiles that are not yet stated are marked as pending before 1.0, so an engine's requests report pending and not the reference's report under the engine's name.
- [U-2026-11-19-d50-cap](#u-2026-11-19-d50-cap) (2026-11-19): The user keeps D50's lift cap at 1.0 for JOIN ... ON conditions and lambda bodies.
- [F73](#f73) (2026-11-18): The finish plan and every incremental maintenance plan are covered by no proof and no contract check; a refresh can raise where the report lists none. R4a docs sentence blocks 1.0; R4b edge test; R4c derived maintenance plans later.
- [F70](#f70) (2026-11-18): #1664 may merge with the exit described in words; step 12e (COUNT bound) is a follow-up and must be on main before the next release carrying #1664's text.
- [U-2026-11-18-python-312-floor](#u-2026-11-18-python-312-floor) (2026-11-18): sverify stays on Python 3.12 and later with no 3.11 support.
- [U-2026-11-18-lake-comparator-after-1-0](#u-2026-11-18-lake-comparator-after-1-0) (2026-11-18): A `lake check`/`lake comparator` gate for sverify is filed as an epic placed after 1.0; no trial is assigned.
- [U-2026-11-16-1485-in-m5](#u-2026-11-16-1485-in-m5) (2026-11-16): The data-engineering value critique epic (#1485) is in milestone M5.
- [U-2026-11-15-engine-free-base-1-0-gate](#u-2026-11-15-engine-free-base-1-0-gate) (2026-11-15): The engine-free base is a 1.0 gate, and main may be unsettled between the steps that reach it.
- [U-2026-11-09-incremental-store-gate](#u-2026-11-09-incremental-store-gate) (2026-11-09): Incremental maintenance ships in 1.0 only if its engine-held store meets a measured size bar for aggregates, joins, windows and distinct. Amended by [U-2026-11-20-io-source-gate](#u-2026-11-20-io-source-gate).
- [U-2026-11-09-engines-optional-extras](#u-2026-11-09-engines-optional-extras) (2026-11-09): Every engine, DuckDB included, is an optional extra, so base sverify is engine-free and verifies plans without running them.
- [U-2026-11-08-snowflake-after-1-0](#u-2026-11-08-snowflake-after-1-0) (2026-11-08): More Snowflake probing, recording and fuzzing is for after 1.0 ("Let's not worry about Snowflake too much").
- [U-2026-11-08-back-in-1-0](#u-2026-11-08-back-in-1-0) (2026-11-08): The abstract domain by kind (#925), the statistics work (#918) and generated Polars and Spark lowerings (#271) move back into 1.0.
- [U-2026-11-05-substrait-execution-out-of-scope](#u-2026-11-05-substrait-execution-out-of-scope) (2026-11-05): Executing exported plans in an outside Substrait consumer is out of scope.
- [U-2026-11-05-quarantine-for-1-0](#u-2026-11-05-quarantine-for-1-0) (2026-11-05): Quarantine is required for 1.0 - a refused batch sets bad rows aside with the reason, lets the rest flow and alerts without stopping the pipeline.
- [U-2026-11-05-dbt-package-reopened](#u-2026-11-05-dbt-package-reopened) (2026-11-05): dbt integration is wanted now, as its own Python package that uses sverify to check dbt contracts and tests statically.
- [U-2026-11-05-dbt-back-in-repo](#u-2026-11-05-dbt-back-in-repo) (2026-11-05): sverify-dbt lives in this repository under dbt/ and is released with sverify at one version.
- [D17](decisions.md#d17-changing-the-reference) (2026-11-04): On 0.x the reference changes where the design needs it, in a minor bump with a changelog fragment; from 1.0 a change is an opening or a narrowing, handled differently. Amended by [F44](#f44).
- [U-2026-11-03-median-quantile-duckdb-first](#u-2026-11-03-median-quantile-duckdb-first) (2026-11-03): Median and quantiles come after 0.4.0, DuckDB first.
- [U-2026-11-02-backlog-close-177-179](#u-2026-11-02-backlog-close-177-179) (2026-11-02): Issues 177, 178 and 179 are closed as not wanted for now; focus stays on verified DuckDB SQL, the reference model and developer experience. Amended by [U-2026-11-05-dbt-package-reopened](#u-2026-11-05-dbt-package-reopened).

## Unclear and reserved

- [D60](#d60), reserved: Reserved for #1285 stage 3b (verified strings). Reserved in ruling 139; no text yet.
- [D63](#d63), reserved: Reserved for substring with a length expression (a general ternary), dropped for now. Reserved in ruling 181.
- [D64](#d64), reserved: Reserved for unnest with offset=, dropped for now. Reserved in ruling 181.
- [D65](#d65), reserved: Reserved for a later binary (BLOB) value kind. Reserved in ruling 186 (B3); ruling 187 names its subject. Float32 constants have no reserved number.
- [D66](#d66), reserved: Reserved for struct casts, a later `Expr.mkStruct`. Reserved in ruling 186; #1978 landed its list half. Open: matching fields by name (DuckDB SQL, D54) or by position (Substrait).
- [D68](#d68), reserved: Reserved for a decimal quantile_cont, refused at 1.0. Reserved in ruling 191.

| <a id="f260"></a>F260 | 2026-11-22 | theory advisor | api | in-force | #2335 meets F258's E2. On a fired run-time check the stage is dropped, dependents wait and the next refresh resumes from the sink. That is the held state users and the pipeline see: `held`, exit 8, nothing committed, the same batch next time. A fired check holds whatever `on_violation` says, because there are no rows to quarantine. A failed condition raises `Held` before anything is applied and keeps its stage. A fired check raises part-way through the plan, so rebuilding the stage from the sink avoids applying part of a batch twice. Conditions: T1, the sink's version and the quarantine table are unchanged after a fired check. T2, a later refresh over a batch that no longer fires commits it, and the sink equals a fresh full run. | <a id="f264"></a>F264 | 2026-11-22 | theory advisor | api | in-force | Refines F263. The refresh JSON is a fourth format that gets a bump. F1's reader for the previous format applies only where sverify reads its own format back (the report JSON and the sink record); the CLI JSON and the refresh JSON bump their version, update their schemas and test the new output against a stored format-1 document under `tests/formats`. The reason/why rule covers the Python API and the JSON sverify writes per run; a stored table's columns and record keys keep their names, so the quarantine record's `reason` and the `_reason` column stay. A reader for a format sverify never reads would be code with no caller. The test for an output-only format checks that the new writer's output differs from the stored format-1 document only in the listed keys, and the changelog fragment names the moved keys for the jobs that read them. Also renamed: `SourceBatch.from_snapshot(reason=)` → `why=`, and the CLI JSON relaxation's `"reason"` → `"why"`, which rides on that format's bump. The quarantine names live in the user's own tables, where users query them with their own SQL, so renaming them would cost a fifth format with a reader and break those queries. | <a id="f265"></a>F265 | 2026-11-22 | theory advisor | architecture | in-force | #1071 PR B's shape. The request carries no observation set: it adds each ensures condition's `reads`, and Lean derives `seen`, `held` and, when the floats are stated, `looser`. The response is keyed by role (`realized`, `seen`, `floats_added`). B is one PR in three commits (B1 the class and evidence, B2 the plan bound and ε meeting, B3 the derived observations), then C. Stated tolerances are compared exactly against the double the user passed, sent as its exact decimal expansion and parsed to a Rat, in a numeric tolerance type local to `Realize.lean`. Applies F257 and settles its open parse question: the tolerance's repr is the nearest short decimal, not the value, so proving against it could accept a bound above the double the run uses. Python's `observed` and the `floats_on` and `evidence_on` calls are deleted. Sending the observation set from Python, or one call per observation, is declined. If B passes about 1,500 changed lines without its pins, it may go as two sequential PRs into main, split only after B2. Until C, `_plan_bound` and `_resolve` stay in Python for the rerun check only. The core is untouched: `FloatObs.within` and `Obs.le` keep their form, since a core tolerance type would change the observation order and need a D-number. No pin is expected to change; one that does goes to Fable with the plan and both values. Conditions: K1, no public surface. K2, `#print axioms` of `Realize.lean`'s theorems stays as it was. K3, the Improvement lines count the Python deleted and the Lean added, and time one `--contract` call on a plan with doubles. K4, the trusted-base row moves to `Realize.lean` in B, and the rerun check's row follows in C. | [#1071](https://github.com/AlexanderNenninger/substrait-lean/issues/1071) |  | fable-ruling-265-1071-pr-b-shape.md |

Every op already has its meaning under D55 and F239, so the reference doesn't change, and there is no new option and no D-number. NaN, ±inf and a product outside i64 raise at the i64 cast, and a count outside the timestamp's range raises at the timestamp cast. Ruling 266 names the scope "frontend", which the schema lacks, so the entry is filed under reference-semantics.
